## MOHAMMED HANIF (ANCESTOR OF THE YIBIRS).

**Kulhídi horimad anghàg ba lagu bidei¹.** (*The time before a priest there was.*) **Hig bu mìdsha², goried la ma midín jirin.** (*Where he lives people with not to live used.*) **Deriïhìni³ horimad, iyo deriïhi angháksodei dehhdodi u mìdshei, limihi ya yíftimeyei.** (*Your people before, and the people (who) prayed (who) among them lived, both fought.*) **Deri yabar ghandiđsan ya la biđei.** (*A man of property plenty he was.*) **Deriïhìni horimad ya u so godisei.** (*Your people before to (him) came.*) **"Awas no ‘idbi," yei laghen.** (*"A herb to us bring," they said.*) **"Khábarke ku feđesan⁴?" yu laghei.** (*"What reason for do you want (it)?" he said.*) **Kulhídas yu laghei, "Deriahan angháksoda yannu ku rùfinena."** (*Then they said, "These people (who) pray we with (it) will kill."*) **Kulhídas yu laghei, "Wa iftimo ghandiđah, idinku i ‘idi mahai⁵, awaskeiga idin ‘idin mayo."** (*Then he said, "It is money plenty you to me give without, my herb to you give (I) will not."*) **Kulhídas yu gođerówi iftimo iyo gođerówi jalmo inhídas àwaski yei kaga doiyòden.** (*Then they a hundred rupees and a hundred camels so much the herb they for bought.*) **Kulhídas yu àwaski u sara ‘idei⁶.** (*Then he the herb to (them) gave.*) **Kulhídas ya deriïhi anghaksóneyei alman so feđten⁷.** (*Then the people (who) prayed a raid went for.*) **Kulhídasa yei deriïhini horimad yei alman u so feđten.** (*Then they your people before they a raid on (them) went for.*) **Kulhídas limihi gođerówi o lawod⁸ yei hig midshei, o higgiu tegèđei ya aimein waiyen.** (*Then for two hundred years they a place lived, and where they went (they) find could not.*) **Kulhidasa** (*Then*)

> ¹ Think. **la biđa** it is thought. This is used for "is" (Somali **wa**).  
> ² Aorist, from **midso**.  
> ³ Plur. **derio**. Here the narrator refers to the people of the person he was addressing (i.e. myself), whom he considers to be the same as the Gala. **deriïhi anghaksodei** means Moslems.  
> ⁴ Somali: **mahhad ku dònesan?**  
> ⁵ Somali: **idinku i sin mahai** unless you give me.  
> ⁶ Hand over. Somali **đib**.  
> ⁷ Look for. (**wan feđta.**) Somali **dòno**. **alman feđo**, Somali **dùl**.  
> ⁸ Water, rains, i.e. year.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 218: Yibir (p. 198) --&gt;
*Page 198*

**deriïhi anghaksóneyei higga ku rùfen.** (*the people (who) prayed there died,*) **Kulhídas yei anghàgi yabar yifmeíyen¹ anghàgi bu rùfei.** (*Then they the priest property fought the priest he died.*) **Àdìsi ya la alman.** (*His home was looted.*) **Weled yahaínyahh u u jagh‘idei, ya higgi ka so godisei, weledki iyo aferi kelemad hig midín jirei.** (*A boy small (whom) he begat, there from came, the boy and four others a place live used to.*) **Weledki Mohammed Hanif ba la biđei.** (*The boy Mohammed Hanif was.*) **Weledku kulhídas yu anghàg nokhdei, asuwàno yu difadín² jirei.** (*The boy then he a priest became, women he used to.*) **Dugagìsu³ higga midsha o asuwàno difadsha², anghàgi yifna ya u só shamei, Au-Bakhardli bu nokhdei.** (*(while) He there lives and women , the priest great to (him) came, Au-Bakhardli he was.*) **Kulhídas yu u laghei, "Khábarma higgo u mìdesa, o anghàg lagugu biđa?"** (*Then he said, "What there for do you live, and a priest for are?"*) **Kulhídas bu laghei, "Dugagàgu ma iga anghaksántahai?"** (*Then he said, "Yourself (are you) me than (more) holy?"*) **Kulhidasu laghei, "Ka angháksanahai."** (*Then he said, "More holy I am."*) **Kulhídas yu u laghei, "Khabarka ad iga anghaksántahai igu aimidsi."** (*Then he said, "The reason you me than (more) holy are me to shew."*) **Kulhídasu laghei, "Higgas an ka⁴ godisaya, kà godis dugagàgu."** (*Then he said, "There I will penetrate, through go yourself."*) **Kulhídasu tomàlaha ànigah yu hosidìsi ka godisei.** (*Then that hill great he beneath it through went.*) **Kulhídas ka godisei, o higgo u ku godisei, yu u laghei Au-Bakhardli, "Tomàlaha O, gan‘id."** (*Then (he) went through, and there he in went, he to him said Au-Bakhardli, "O Hill, seize."*) **Kulhídas tomàlihi 'ss ku godisei, kulhídas yu hig u ka so godiso aimein wai.** (*Then the hill together went, then he where he out may come see could not.*) **Tomàlaha dehhdìsi yu ku rùfei** (*The hill in it he died*)

> ¹ This is not correctly given, but the sense is "They fought over the dead priest's property."  
> ² Whether this means "marry," or "rape" is not clear. **difad** rope. There is one story that Mohammed Hanif was expelled by Sheik Ishhak because of his immorality.  
> ³ Self, person. **dugagaìga** I myself.  
> ⁴ Through, across.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 219: Mohammed Hanif (p. 199) --&gt;
*Page 199*

**anghàgi.** (*the priest.*) **Anghàgi aïháyaga higgas u ku rùfei.** (*The priest of our tribe there he died.*) **Kulhídas ya weldihi¹ u jagh‘idei yu u laghei, "Augayo ada rùfìyei, agar no-ga² ‘id."** (*Then the boys he begat they said, "Our father you have killed, something to us for it give."*) **Anghàgi ba ku laghei, khabarkan kulhídasu u laghei, "Ma watahhádan gođerówi jalmo idin ‘ida, mase weledki goried u jagh‘ido yan ilbir idin ka sara ‘ida? Saradoshíski mian iftin idin ka sara ‘ida?"** (*The priest to (them) said, this word then he said, "(Am I) to-day a hundred camels to you to give, or the son a Somali begets I a ewe to you for (him) am I to give? The marriage am I money to you for to give?"*) **Kulhídas ya weldihi laghen, "Weledka ilbir noga sara ‘id, saradoshíska iftin, wéldahana³ ilbir. Inhídi ka darseisa inhída khabarka yannu ágarta ku ‘idónena."** (*Then the boys said, "The boy a ewe to us for (him) give, the marriage money, and the boys a ewe. That (which) follows (hereafter) so much for that we as the price for will receive."*) **Khabarkas yannu ágar ku shansonna, Anàsyodáyadu.** (*For that reason we a price take, we Yibirs.*) **Kulhídi iftinta iyo ilbirta na lo ‘ido, àwasyo yahaínyahh yannu u ‘idna. Wannu u yabarónna.** (*When the money and the ewe to us is given, sticks small we to them give. We thus earn our living.*) **Àwas kelemad o ghandìđah wa ku duhùrna.** (*Herbs other many (we) understood.*) **Derigi rùfrùfeya iyo derigi alman feđóneya, iyo derigi lagheya, "an derigas ka ur behhénsanàdo," inhídas àwas lo ‘ido yannu ku duhùrna.** (*The man (who) is sick and the man (who) is going on a raid, and the man (who) says, "let me than that man be better," for that a herb to be given we know.*) **Deriga, annu u ‘idna, iftimo ghandìđah yu, kulhída u yifnàdo, no sara ‘ida.** (*That man, (to whom) we give, money plenty he, when he is successful, to us hands.*)

> ¹ Plur. **weldo-hi.**  
> ² **na u ka.** Cf. **ka sìso** pay for.  
> ³ And.

&lt;!-- PDF Page 220: Yibir-English and Midgan-English Vocabulary (p. 200) --&gt;
*Page 200*

# YIBIR-ENGLISH AND MIDGAN-ENGLISH VOCABULARY.

The following is a list of Yibir and Midgan words not used by other Somalis.

Words, such as Pronouns, Particles, etc., are not given, being common to all three dialects.

Nouns are recognised by the Definite Article which follows each noun, separated by a hyphen.

*Examples*,
- **ain-ti** eye &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; **bulal-ki** fire

In these examples, **ain** equals an eye, **bulal** equals a fire; "the eye," "the fire," would be, **ainti, bulalki**.

The suffixes, **-ki, -gi, -hi**, are masculine, **-ti, -di**, are feminine.

Abbreviations:
- (Y) — Yibir dialect.
- (M) — Midgan dialect.
- (Y), (M) — common to both dialects.
- v.i. — intransitive verb.
- v.t. — transitive verb.
- a. — adjective.

The Arabic letter ain (**ع**) is represented by **‘**, ghain is represented by **gh, kh**.

**đ** represents the "cerebral d," which at the beginning or end of a word sounds like *d*, but in the middle of a word is more like *r*.

This letter in Yibir is pronounced usually like **dh**.

- **à-di** (pl. **àö-hi**) (Y), family, "rer," possessions
- **abàbo-di** (M), Plateau Gazelle, "dero"
- **Àbir-ki** (Y), Tomal (an outcast tribe that work in iron)
- **adeisímo-di** (M), milk
- **áferi-hi** (Y), four
- **afjaghin-ti** (M), mouth
- **ágar-ti** (Y), thing, any concrete object; *agarma ku midesa?* what is there?
- **aghtul** v.t. (M), strike, hit
- **aimei** v.t. (Y), see, find, understand
- **aimeisi** v.t. (Y), shew, teach
- **ain-ti** (Y), eye; *ainta ku yef*, turn your eye (i.e. look)
- **aintoli-hi** (Y), lie, untruth

# COMPARATIVE VOCABULARY OF SOMALI, YIBIR, AND MIDGAN.

| *English* | *Somali* | *Yibir* | *Midgan* |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| after | **dambe** | **darsad** | **dibyalin** |
| amulet | **ghordas-ki** | **godahheđ-ki** | |
| animal | **báhal-ki** | **dálanga-hi** | **hangagùri-gi** |
| anna | **gambo-di** | **madôkushan-ki** | |
| 2 annas | **antìn-ti** | **yahan-ti** | |
| arm | **ga‘an-ti** | **gànad-di** | **farolahàto-di** |
| army | **‘oll-ki** | **alman-ki** | **moyo-di** |
| arrow | **fallàd-di** | **wafèro yahainyahh** | **đegoyir** |
| backbone | **ađahh-hi** | **hawar-ti** | |
| bad | **hhun** | **‘urshèn** | **neghatal** |
| be | **aho** | **la biđ** (be thought) | |
| bear (beget) | **đal** | **jagh‘id** | **finfin** |
| before | **hor** | **horyad** | **horyalin** |
| beg | **bari** | **gorbei** | |
| belly | **leg-gi** | **labodin-ki** | **labodin-ki** |
| beneath | **hòs** | **hosyad** | **hosyalin** |
| bird | **shimbir-ti** | **haman-ti** | **alèliso-di** |
| black | **madô** | **humăksan** | **neghatal** |
| blood | **đìg-gi** | **‘asèro-hi** | **‘asowa-hi** |
| bone | **laf-ti** | **lafil-shi** | **lafeiti-di** |
| bow | **ghànso-di** | **ildighan-ti** | **sukhodin-ti** |
| boy | **wil-ki** | **weled-ki** | **janakh-i** |
| bradawl | **muda‘-i** | **mukhtaren-ki** | |
| breast | **lab-ti** | **d‘arowa-hi** | **fèđolahàto-di** |
| bring | **la kàli / so kahhai** | **so shimi** | **so shamei / so ‘idbi** |
| burden (of camel) | **ákhal-ki** | **kabàr-ti** | **yagòl-ki** |
| bush | **gèd-ki / đir-ti** | **awas-ki** | **ghoribirro-di** |
| buy | **ìbso** | **doïyo** | **dahhbi** |
| butter | **bur‘ad-di** | **buskulohh-i** | |

&lt;!-- PDF Page 234: Comparative Vocabulary — Numbers &amp; Wild Animals (p. 214) --&gt;
*Page 214*

### NUMBERS.

| *English* | *Somali* | *Yibir* | *Midgan* |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| one | **mid** | **deri** (= finger) | |
| two | **laba** | **limihi** | |
| three | **sadehh** | **sadehhi** | |
| four | **afar** | **aferi** | |
| five | **shan** | **gànad** (= hand) | |
| six | **lehh** | **gànad iyo deri** | |
| ten | **toban** | **limihi gànadod**, or **tobanihi** | |
| fifteen | **shanyo-toban** | **sadehhi gànadod** | |
| hundred | **boghol** | **gođerowi-gi** (= rosary of 100 beads) | |
| thousand | **kun** | **tobanihi gođerowiyod** | |

The Midgans use the Yibir numbers up to ten.

### NAMES OR DESCRIPTIONS OF WILD ANIMALS.

| *English* | *Somali* | *Yibir* | *Midgan* |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Caracal | **jambèl** | | **jimikh** |
| Cheetah | **harimad** | | **‘unimadô** |
| Dikdik | **sagàro** | **d.\* yahainyahha awaski ka godisa** | **bikho** |
| Elephant | **maròdi** | | **dusár** |
| Fox | **da‘wo** | **d. dađo ‘ita** | **humbur** |
| Gazelle, Clarke's | **dibotag** | **d. darasti tegèja** | **dibodèr** |
| Gazelle, Soemmering's | **‘aul** | **d. darasti iftimaleh** | **gosinki / (buck) jehhar** |
| Gazelle, Speke's | **dèro** | **d. amèđo la hega** | **abàbo** |
| Gazelle, Waller's | **gerenùk** | **d. la bilehhoda** | **tingir / geryal / (buck) lig** |
| Guinea-fowl | **digirin** | | **oran** |
| Haartebeest | **sig** | **d. albakharki** | **dabo‘ad** |
| Hyaena | **waràba** | **d. khábarki ghandiđsanleh** | **furat** |
| Koodoo | **aderyo** | | **gođir** |
| Leopard | **shabèl** | **d. amèđo ‘ita** | **dussi** |
| Lion | **libahh** | **d. jalmo ‘ita** | **hangagùri ghàn** |
| Oryx | **b‘e‘id** | **d. walahumo ku dashìya** | **gesođer / gesolahato / (buck) saryen** |
| herd of Oryx | | | **yahhab-ki** |
| Ostrich | **gorei / halda** | | **hedig / gir / sareyagh** |
| O. feather | **bàl** | | **rish** |
| Rhinoceros | **wìyil** | | **amadur** |
| Tortoise | **dìn** | **makabùr** | **madôla** |

> \* *Note.* **d** (in Yibir) represents **dálanga** animal.
